Foreign exchange risk
The Group mainly operates in the PRC with most of the transactions          外匯風險
settled in Renminbi. The management of the Group considers that             本集團主要於中國營運，大部分交易以人民幣結
the business is not exposed to any significant foreign exchange             算。本集團管理層認為，業務並不面臨任何重大
risk as it has no significant financial assets or liabilities that are      外匯風險，因為本集團並無重大金融資產或負債
denominated in currencies other than the respective functional              是以本集團經營實體各自功能貨幣以外的貨幣計
currencies of its operating entities. The Group did not hedge against       值。於報告期間內，本集團並無對沖外幣匯率的
any fluctuation in exchange rates of foreign currency during the            任何波動。
Reporting Period.

During FY2023, the Group recorded foreign exchange losses of
RMB3.2 million (FY2022: foreign exchange losses of RMB9.9                   於 2023 財 年，本 集 團 錄 得 外 匯 虧 損 人 民 幣 3.2 百
million), which was mainly arising from bank balances held by               萬元（2022 財年：外匯虧損人民幣 9.9 百萬元），
offshore subsidiaries of the Company denominated in Renminbi as a           主要由本公司的境外附屬公司持有的銀行結餘（以
result of the devaluation of Renminbi to Hong Kong dollars during the       人民幣計值）因報告期間人民幣兌港元貶值引起。
Reporting Period.

Share Option Scheme                                                         購股權計劃
The Share Option Scheme was adopted by the Shareholders on 19               購股權計劃於 2023 年 6 月 19 日獲股東採納，惟須
June 2023, conditional upon the listing of the Shares on the main           待股份於聯交所主板上市後方可作實，並自上市
board of the Stock Exchange and effective from the Listing Date.            日期起生效。

The purpose of the Share Option Scheme is to incentivize and                購股權計劃之目的為激勵及獎勵該計劃項下的合
reward the eligible persons under the scheme for their contribution         資格人士對本集團作出的貢獻，讓彼等利益與本
to the Group and to align their interests with that of the Company as       公司的利益掛鈎，以鼓勵彼等致力於提高本公司
so to encourage them to work towards enhancing the value of the             價值。合資格參與購股權計劃的人士為 (i) 本集團
Company. Persons eligible to participate the Share Option Scheme            任何成員公司的任何董事及僱員；(ii) 本集團任何
are (i) any director and employee of any member of the Group; (ii)          控股公司、同系附屬公司或聯營公司的任何董事
any director or employee of any of the holding companies, fellow            或 僱 員；及 (iii) 任 何 人 士（包 括 一 名 實 體）於 其 一
subsidiaries or associated companies of the Group; and (iii) any            般及正常業務過程中持續或經常性地向本集團提
person (including an entity) that provides services to the Group on         供符合其長期增長利益的服務（「服務提供商」）。
a continuing or recurring basis in its ordinary and usual course of
business which are in the interests of its long term growth (the “Service
Provider(s)”).

The Share Option Scheme will remain valid and effective for a period        購股權計劃將自上市日期起計 10 年內有效及生效，
of 10 years from the Listing Date and shall expire on 12 July 2033. As      並將於 2033 年 7 月 12 日屆滿。於本報告日期，購
at the date of this report, the Share Option Scheme had a remaining         股權計劃之剩餘年期約為 9 年零 4 個月。
life of approximately 9 years and 4 months.

The total number of Shares which may be allotted and issued upon            根據本集團採納的購股權計劃及任何其他購股權
exercise of all options to be granted under the Share Option Scheme         計劃所授出全部購股權獲行使後而可能配發及發
and any other share option scheme adopted by the Group must not             行的股份總數，合共不得超過截至上市日期已發
in aggregate exceed 10% of the total number of Shares in issue as of        行股份總數的 10%（「計劃授權限額」）。在計劃授
the Listing Date (the “Scheme Mandate Limit”). Within the Scheme            權限額內，因行使將授予服務提供商的所有購股

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE CODE                                                 企業管治守則
The Group is dedicated to implementing high standards of corporate        本集團致力於實施高標準的企業管治，以保障股
governance in order to safeguard the interests of the Shareholders        東利益，提升企業價值。本公司已採納上市規則
and enhance its corporate value. The Company has adopted the CG           附錄 C1 所載的企業管治守則作為其企業管治守則。
Code as set out in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules as its own code
of corporate governance.

The Board is committed to the Company’s objective of consistent           董事會致力於實現本公司持續增長及提升股東價
growth and increase in Shareholder value. The Board has the               值的目標。董事會共同負責制定本公司的業務戰
collective responsibility to formulate the Company’s business             略，監督其運營及財務表現，以促進本公司的成功，
strategies and monitor its operational and financial performances         實現長期財務穩定及回報。
in order to promote the Company’s success and achieve long-term
financial stability and return.

The Board recognizes that corporate governance should be the              董事會認識到，企業管治應屬董事的集體責任，
collective responsibility of Directors and their principal role and       彼等於企業管治方面的主要角色及職能為制定及
function in relation to corporate governance is to formulate and review   審查本公司的企業管治政策及常規，審查及監督
the Company’s policies and practices on corporate governance,             本公司董事及高級管理層的培訓及持續專業發展，
to review and monitor the training and continuous professional            審查及監督本公司遵守法律及監管規定方面的政
development of Directors and senior management of the Company,            策及常規，制定、審查及監督適用於僱員及董事
to review and monitor the Company’s policies and practices on             的行為守則，及審查本公司的企業管治守則合規
compliance with legal and regulatory requirements, to formulate,          情況，及載於本公司年報的本公司企業管治報告
review and monitor the code of conduct applicable to employee and         中的披露。
Directors, and to review the Company’s compliance with the CG Code
and disclosure in the corporate governance report of the Company
under the annual report of the Company.

To the knowledge of the Directors, from the Listing Date until 31         據 董 事 所 知，自 上 市 日 期 起 至 2023 年 12 月 31 日
December 2023 and up to the date of this report, the Company has          及直至本報告日期，本公司已遵守企業管治守則

BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY                                                     董事會多元化政策
The Board has adopted a board diversity policy which sets out              董事會已採納董事會多元化政策，當中載列實現
the approach to achieve diversity on the Board. The Company                董事會多元化的方法。本公司深知設有多元化董
recognizes the importance and benefits of having a diverse Board           事會的重要性及裨益，並視董事會層面日益多元
and sees increasing diversity at Board level as an essential element       化為實現本公司戰略目標及可持續發展的要素。
in achieving the Company’s strategic goals and sustainable                 本公司務求通過考慮眾多因素以實現董事會多元
development. The Company seeks to achieve Board diversity through          化，該等因素包括但不限於專業知識、技能、經驗、
the consideration of a number of factors, including but not limited to     獨 立 性、年 齡、文 化、種 族 及 性 別。本 公 司 將 考
expertise, skills, experience, independence, age, culture, ethnicity       慮董事會多元化政策及其他因素，同時基於對董
and gender. The Company will select potential Board candidates             事會的潛在貢獻挑選潛在董事會候選人。董事會
based on his or her potential contribution to the Board while taking       的所有委任均以用人唯才為原則，並在充分顧及
into account the board diversity policy and other factors. All Board       董事會多元化的裨益後以客觀標準考慮候選人。
appointments will be based on meritocracy and candidates will

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL                                       風險管理及內部控制
The Board acknowledges that it has overall responsibility for the          董事會承認，其全面負責內部控制及風險管理的
design and implementation of internal controls and risk management         設計及實施，涵蓋本公司的財務報告、營運、合
which cover financial reporting, operations, compliance and risk           規及風險管理，並持續監控該等內部控制及風險
management of the Company, as well as continuous monitoring the            管理的有效性。
effectiveness of such internal controls and risk management.

The Company has established risk management and internal control           本公司已設立風險管理及內部控制系統，包括其
systems consisting of policies and procedures that it considers            認為對其業務營運屬適當的政策及程序。有關系
to be appropriate for its business operations. Such systems are            統旨在管理而非消除無法實現業務目標的風險，
designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to            只能合理而非絕對保證不會出現重大誤報或損失。
achieve business objectives, and can only provide reasonable and           董事會授權審核委員會在本公司高級管理層的協
not absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss. The          助下，持續監督及審查風險管理及內部控制制度

unclaimed shall be forfeited and shall revert to the Company in

We also provide the directors with a statement that we have complied    我們還向董事提交聲明，說明我們已符合有關獨
with relevant ethical requirements regarding independence, and to       立性的相關專業道德要求，並與彼等溝通有可能
communicate with them all relationships and other matters that may      合理地被認為會影響我們獨立性的所有關係和其
reasonably be thought to bear on our independence, and where            他事項，以及在適用的情況下，為消除威脅而採
applicable, actions taken to eliminate threats or safeguards applied.   取的行動或防範措施。

From the matters communicated with the directors, we determine          從與董事溝通的事項中，我們確定哪些事項對本
those matters that were of most significance in the audit of the        期間綜合財務報表的審核最為重要，因而構成關
consolidated financial statements of the current period and are         鍵審核事項。我們在核數師報告中描述該等事項，
therefore the key audit matters. We describe these matters in our       除非法律或法規不允許公開披露該等事項，或在
auditor’s report unless law or regulation precludes public disclosure   極端罕見的情況下，如果合理預期在我們報告中
about the matter or when, in extremely rare circumstances, we           溝通某事項造成的負面後果超過產生的公眾利益，
determine that a matter should not be communicated in our report        我們決定不應在報告中溝通該事項。
because the adverse consequences of doing so would reasonably
be expected to outweigh the public interest benefits of such

    68 Jinjie Road, Huaqiao Town, Kunshan, China.                          路 68 號夢星園 1 座 2 樓。

    Company and its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”) are               屬 公 司（統 稱「本 集 團」）主 要 於 中 華 人 民 共
    principally engaged in new retail business and IP creation             和 國（「中 國」）從 事 新 零 售 業 務 以 及 IP 創 造

    Mr. Yang, Chun-Jung (“Mr. Yang”), Ms. Yeh, Hui-Mei (“Ms.               先 生（「楊 先 生」）、葉 惠 美 女 士（「葉 女 士」）

3     FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                            3     財務風險管理
3.1 Financial risk factors                                                 3.1 財務風險因素
      The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks:          本集團業務面臨多種財務風險：市場風險（包
      market risk (including foreign exchange risk, cash flow and                括外匯風險、現金流量及公平值利率風險以
      fair value interest rate risk and price risk), credit risk and             及價格風險）、信貸風險及流動資金風險。
      liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk management programme              本集團的整體風險管理計劃專注於金融市
      focuses on the unpredictability of financial markets and seeks             場的不可預測性，並尋求盡量降低對本集團
      to minimise potential adverse effects on the Group’s financial             財務表現可能構成的不利影響。
      performance.

      Financial risk management is carried out by the finance                    財務風險管理在董事會監督下由財務部執行。
      department under the supervision of the board of directors.                董事會制訂整體風險管理的原則。
      The board provides principles for overall risk management.

(a)   Market risk                                                          (a)   市場風險
(i)   Foreign exchange risk                                                (i)   外匯風險

      transactions or recognised assets and liabilities are denominated          集團實體功能貨幣以外的貨幣計值時，外匯
      in a currency that is not the Group entities’ functional currency.         風險即會產生。

      The Group manages its foreign exchange risk by closely                     本集團透過密切監察外匯匯率的變動管理
      monitoring the movement of the foreign currency rates.                     其外匯風險。從中國匯出的現金須遵守中國
      Cash repatriation from the PRC are subject to the rules and                政府頒佈的外匯管制規則及條例。本集團並
      regulations of foreign exchange control promulgated by the                 無其他重大外匯風險。
      PRC government. The Group did not have other significant
      exposure to foreign exchange risk.

      Credit risk is managed on a group basis. The credit risk of                 信貸風險按組合基準管理。本集團的信貸風
      the Group mainly arises from financial assets, cash and cash                險主要來自金融資產、現金及現金等價物、
      equivalents, restricted bank deposits and trade and other                   受限制銀行存款以及貿易及其他應收款項。
      receivables. The carrying amounts of these balances represent               該等結餘的賬面值指本集團就該等資產所
      the Group’s maximum exposure to credit risk in relation to                  面臨的最大信貸風險。
      these assets.

      In order to minimise the credit risk, management has delegated              為盡量降低信貸風險，管理層已委派一支負
      a team responsible for determination of credit limits, credit               責確定信貸限額、信貸審批及其他監督程序
      approvals and other monitoring procedures to ensure that                    的團隊，以確保採取跟進措施收回逾期債務。
      follow-up action is taken to recover overdue debts. Before                  於接納新債務人前，本集團會評估潛在債務
      accepting any new debtor, the Group assesses the potential                  人的信貸質素並界定債務人的信貸限額。本
      debtor’s credit quality and defines credit limits by debtor. Credit         集團定期審閱授予債務人的信貸限額。此外，
      limits granted to debtors are reviewed regularly. In addition,              本集團於報告期末審閱各個別債項的可收
      the Group reviews the recoverable amount of each individual                 回金額，確保已就不可收回金額計提足夠的
      debt at the end of the reporting period to ensure that adequate             減值虧損。就此而言，管理層認為本集團的
      impairment losses are made for irrecoverable amounts. In this               信貸風險大大降低。
      regard, management considers that the Group’s credit risk is
      significantly reduced.

      As at 31 December 2023 and 2022, all the bank deposits were                 於 2023 年 及 2022 年 12 月 31 日，所 有 銀 行
      deposited in or managed by state-owned or reputable banks                   存款乃存放於國有銀行或具聲譽的銀行（均
      which are all high-credit-quality financial institutions without            為並無重大信貸風險的所有高信貸質素金
      significant credit risk.                                                    融機構）或由其管理。

       expected credit losses prescribed by HKFRS 9, which permits               第 9 號規定就預期信貸虧損計提撥備，該準
       the use of the lifetime expected credit loss provision for all            則允許對所有貿易應收款項採用全期預期虧
       trade receivables. To measure the expected credit losses, trade           損撥備。為計量預期信貸虧損，貿易應收款
       receivables have been grouped based on shared credit risk                 項已根據共同信貸風險特徵及發票日期分組。
       characteristics and the invoice dates.

       individual basis or grouped based on shared credit risk                   險特徵及逾期天數分類評估預期信貸風險。
       characteristics and the days past due. The assessment is                  評估乃根據客戶的背景及信譽、歷史償還記
       based on the background and reputation of the customers,                  錄及過往經驗。管理層亦考慮外部評級機構
       historical settlement records and past experience. Management             報告指出的違約率及違約損失率，以及可能
       also considered the default rates and loss given default from             影響客戶償還未償還結餘的能力的前瞻性
       external rating agency report and forward-looking information             資料。與已知面臨財務困難或對收回應收款
       that may impact the customer’s ability to repay the outstanding           項產生重大疑慮的貿易應收款項被視為面
       balances. Trade receivables with known financial difficulties             臨較大違約風險，須單獨進行測試。
       or significant doubt on collection of receivables are considered
       to be subjected to higher risk of default and are tested
       individually.

       On that basis, the loss allowance as at 31 December 2023                  按 該 基 準，於 2023 年 及 2022 年 12 月 31 日
       and 2022 was determined for trade receivables, and the                    釐定貿易應收款項虧損準備，且下述預期信

3    FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT (Continued)                                3   財務風險管理（續）
3.2 Capital risk management                                               3.2 資本風險管理
     The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard            本集團管理資金的目標旨在保障本集團按
     the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order              持續經營基準繼續營運的能力，以為股東帶
     to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other               來回報，同時兼顧其他利益相關方的利益，
     stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to             並維持最佳的資本架構以減低資本成本。
     reduce the cost of capital.

     In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group          為維持或調整資本架構，本集團可能調整向
     may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders,                 股東支付的股息金額、向股東退回資本、發
     return capital to shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets          行新股份或出售資產，從而減少債務。
     to reduce debt.

     The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing ratio.            本集團根據資本負債比率監控資本。該比率
     This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital. Net       按債務淨額除以總資本計算。債務淨額按借
     debt is calculated as total borrowings, financial instrument             款總額、附帶贖回權利的金融工具、租賃負
     with redemption rights, lease liabilities, amounts due to related        債、應付關聯方款項及應付第三方款項減現
     parties and amounts due to third parties less cash and cash              金及現金等價物以及受限制銀行存款計算。
     equivalents and restricted bank deposits. Total capital is               總 資 本 按 綜 合 財 務 狀 況 表 所 示 的「權 益」加
     calculated as “equity” as shown in the consolidated statement            債務淨額計算。
     of financial position plus net debt.

     The gearing ratios at 31 December 2023 and 2022 were as                  於 2023 年 及 2022 年 12 月 31 日 的 資 本 負 債
     follows:                                                                 比率如下：

                                                                                               於 12 月 31 日

3.3 Fair value estimation                                                  3.3 公平值估計
     This section explains the judgments and estimates made in                 本節闡述釐定於財務報表中確認及按公平
     determining the fair values of the financial instruments that             值計量的金融工具公平值所作出的判斷及
     are recognised and measured at fair value in the financial                估計。為得出有關釐定公平值所用輸入數據
     statements. To provide an indication about the reliability of             的可靠性指標，本集團將其金融工具分為三
     the inputs used in determining fair value, the Group has                  個層級：
     categorised its financial instruments into three levels as follows:

     Level 1:    The fair value of financial instruments traded in             第一層： 在活躍市場上買賣的金融工具（如
                 active markets (such as publicly traded derivatives                公開買賣的衍生工具及股本證券）
                 and equity securities) is based on quoted market                   的公平值乃按於報告期末的市場
                 prices at the end of the reporting period. The                     報價釐定。本集團所持有金融資
                 quoted market price used for financial assets                      產採用的市場報價為當時買盤價。
                 held by the Group is the current bid price. These                  該等工具計入第一層。
                 instruments are included in level 1.

     Level 2:    The fair value of financial instruments that are not          第二層： 未在活躍市場買賣的金融工具（例
                 traded in an active market (for example, over-the-                 如場外衍生工具）的公平值利用
                 counter derivatives) is determined using valuation                 估值技術釐定，該等估值技術盡
                 techniques which maximise the use of observable                    量利用可觀察市場數據，並盡量
                 market data and rely as little as possible on entity-              減少依賴實體特定估計。倘計量
                 specific estimates. If all significant inputs required             工具公平值所需所有重大輸入數
                 to fair value an instrument are observable, the                    據均可觀察，則該工具計入第二
                 instrument is included in level 2.                                 層。

     Level 3:    If one or more of the significant inputs is not based         第三層： 倘一項或多項重大輸入數據並非
                 on observable market data, the instrument is                       根據可觀察市場數據得出，則該
                 included in level 3. This is the case for investment               工具計入第三層。對於並無可觀
                 in certain WMPs without observable market data.                    察市場數據的若干理財產品投資
                                                                                    而言亦是如此。

(ii)   Valuation inputs and relationships to fair value                 (ii)   估值輸入數據及與公平值的關係
       The fair value assessment methods and related key assumptions           本集團管理層採用的公平值評估方法以及
       and judgments adopted by the Group’s management is income               相關關鍵假設及判斷為收益法（具體為現金
       approach (specifically, discounted cash flow method): Uses              流量貼現法）：採用估值技術將未來金額（具
       valuation techniques to convert future amounts (specifically,           體為基於預期回報率的現金流量）轉換為現
       cash flows based on the expected rate of return) to a present           值。
       amount.

       During the year ended 31 December 2023, the Group                       截至 2023 年 12 月 31 日止年度，本集團向持

(d)   Accounting policies of revenue recognition                         (d)   收入確認的會計政策
      Revenue is recognised when or as the control of goods or                 收入於貨品或服務的控制權轉移至客戶時
      services is transferred to the customer. Depending on the terms          確認。視乎合約條款及適用於該合約的法律
      of the contract and the laws that apply to the contract, revenue         規定，收入可隨時間或於某一時間點確認。
      may be recognised over time or at a point in time.

      Control of the good or service is transferred over time if the           倘本集團的履約符合下列其中一項情況，貨
      Group’s performance:                                                     品或服務的控制權將隨時間轉移：

      –     provides all of the benefits received and consumed                 －   提供所有獲客戶同時收取並耗用的利
            simultaneously by the customer;                                        益；

      –     creates or enhances an asset that the customer controls            －   因本集團履約而產生或增加客戶所控
            as the Group performs; or                                              制的資產；或

      –     does not create an asset with an alternative use to                －   並無產生對本集團而言有其他用途的
            the Group and the Group has an enforceable right to                    資 產，而 本 集 團 可 強 制 執 行 其 權 利 收
            payment for performance completed to date.                             回至今已完成履約的款項。

(d)   Accounting policies of revenue recognition (Continued)              (d)   收入確認的會計政策（續）
      If control of the goods and services transfers over time, revenue         倘貨品及服務的控制權隨時間轉移，則收入
      is recognised over the period of the contract by reference to             按合約期間參考已完成履約責任的進度確認。
      the progress towards complete satisfaction of that performance            否則，收入於客戶獲得貨品及服務控制權的
      obligation. Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time           某一時間點確認。
      when the customer obtains control of the goods and services.

      The progress towards complete satisfaction of the performance             已完成履約責任的進度按最能描述本集團
      obligation is measured based on time-based measure of                     履行履約責任表現的以時間為基礎的進度
      progress that best depicts the Group’s performance in satisfying          計量方式計量。
      the performance obligation.

      Contracts with customers may include multiple performance                 與客戶訂立的合約可能包含多項履約責任。
      obligations. For such arrangements, the Group allocates                   就該等安排而言，本集團按其相對獨立的售
      revenue to each performance obligation based on its relative              價為每項履約責任分配收入。本集團一般根
      standalone selling price. The Group generally determines                  據向客戶收取的價格釐定獨立售價。倘獨立
      standalone selling prices based on the prices charged to                  售價無法直接觀察得出，則會視乎可觀察資
      customers. If the standalone selling price is not directly                料的可用性，使用預計成本加利潤或經調整
      observable, it is estimated using expected cost plus a margin             市場評估法進行估算。在估算各項不同履約
      or adjusted market assessment approach, depending on                      責任的相對售價時已作出假設及估計，倘更
      the availability of observable information. Assumptions and               改對該等假設及估計的判斷，則可能會影響
      estimations have been made in estimating the relative selling             收入確認。
      price of each distinct performance obligation, and changes in
      judgments on these assumptions and estimates may impact
      the revenue recognition.

      When either party to a contract has performed, the Group                  當合約的任何一方已履約時，本集團會根據
      presents the contract in the consolidated statement of financial          實體履約與客戶付款之間的關係，將綜合財
      position as a contract asset or a contract liability, depending           務狀況表中的合約呈列為合約資產或合約
      on the relationship between the entity’s performance and the              負債。
      customer’s payment.

      A contract asset is the Group’s right to consideration in                 合約資產為本集團就換取本集團已轉移至
      exchange for goods and services that the Group has transferred            客戶的貨品及服務收取代價的權利。當本集
      to a customer. A receivable is recorded when the Group has                團擁有無條件收取代價的權利時，會將應收
      an unconditional right to consideration. A right to consideration         款項入賬。倘只需要經過一段時間代價即到
      is unconditional if only the passage of time is required before           期應付，則收取代價的權利屬無條件。
      payment of the consideration is due.

11 EARNINGS PER SHARE                                                        11 每股盈利
(a)   Basic earnings per share                                               (a)   每股基本盈利
      Basic earnings per share is calculated by dividing the profit                於截至 2023 年 12 月 31 日止年度，每股基本
      attributable to equity holders of the Company by the weighted                盈利按本公司權益持有人應佔溢利除以發
      average numbers of ordinary shares outstanding during the                    行在外的普通股加權平均數計算（2022 年：
      year ended 31 December 2023 (2022: same).                                    相同）。

      The weighted average number of ordinary shares used for the                  用 於 計 算 截 至 2023 年 及 2022 年 12 月 31 日
      purpose of calculating the basic earning per share for the years             止年度每股基本盈利的普通股加權平均數，
      ended 31 December 2023 and 2022 have been retrospectively                    已就附註 24(a) 所述於 2023 年 6 月 19 日進行
      adjusted for the effects of the capitalisation issue of the ordinary         的普通股資本化發行的影響進行追溯調整。
      shares which took place on 19 June 2023 as set out in Note                   計 算 截 至 2023 年 及 2022 年 12 月 31 日 止 年
      24(a). The shares were deemed to have been allocated and                     度的每股基本及攤薄盈利時，股份被視為自

          the statutory surplus reserve accumulated to more than                 止撥付。
          50% of the registered capital. Certain subsidiaries have
          reached such ceilings.

          Statutory reserve can be used to make up the loss or                   獲 得 主 管 當 局 審 批 後，法 定 儲 備 可 用
          increase the paid-in capital after approval from the                   於彌補虧損或增加實繳資本。
          appropriate authorities.

          currency of RMB for the financial statements of the
          Group.

36 RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS AND BALANCES                           36 關聯方交易及結餘
   Parties are considered to be related if one party has the           倘一方有能力直接或間接控制另一方，或在
   ability, directly or indirectly, to control the other party or      作出財務及經營決策方面能對另一方行使
   exercise significant influence over the other party in making       重大影響力，則雙方被視為關聯方。倘雙方
   financial and operating decisions. Parties are also considered      受共同控制、共同重大影響或聯合控制，亦
   to be related if they are subject to common control, common         被視為關聯方。
   significant influence or joint control.

   As mentioned in note 1, the Company is controlled by the            誠 如 附 註 1 所 述，本 公 司 由 控 股 股 東（即 馬
   controlling shareholders, being Ms. Ma, Mr. Yang, Ms. Yeh and       女 士、楊 先 生、葉 女 士 及 陳 先 生）控 制，彼
   Mr. Chen, who acted in concert pursuant to a concert party          等根據一致行動人士協議一致行動。各控股
   agreement. Each of the controlling shareholder, members of          股東、主要管理成員及其近親家庭成員（包
   key management and their close family members, including            括由彼等控制或可行使重大影響力的公司）
   companies controlled by them or they can exercise significant       均被視為本集團的關聯方。
   influence, are considered as related parties of the Group.

   In the opinion of the directors, the related party transactions     董事認為，關聯方交易乃於正常業務過程中
   were carried out in normal course of business and at terms          按本集團與各關聯方磋商之條款進行。
   negotiated between the Group and the respective related
   parties.

40 SUMMARY OF OTHER POTENTIALLY MATERIAL                                     40 其他可能重大會計政策概要
   ACCOUNTING POLICIES
40.1 Subsidiaries                                                            40.1 附屬公司
      Subsidiaries are all entities over which the Group has control.           附屬公司指本集團擁有控制權的所有實體。
      The Group controls an entity where the Group is exposed to,               倘本集團因參與實體營運而承擔風險或有
      or has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with the          權享有當中的可變動回報，且能夠透過其管
      entity and has the ability to affect those returns through its            理該實體業務的權力影響該等回報，則本集
      power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are fully      團控制該實體。附屬公司自控制權轉移至本
      consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to             集團之日起全面綜合入賬，自控制權終止日
      the Group. They are deconsolidated from the date that control             期起終止綜合入賬。
      ceases.

      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for               本集團使用會計收購法為並非共同控制下
      business combinations not under common control by the                     的業務合併入賬。
      Group.

      Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains                 公司間交易、結餘及集團公司間交易的未變
      on transactions between group companies are eliminated.                   現收益均予對銷。除非交易提供證據顯示所
      Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless the transaction              轉讓資產已減值，否則未變現虧損亦會對銷。
      provides evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.              附屬公司的會計政策已於必要時作出更改，
      Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed where               確保與本集團所採納的政策一致。
      necessary to ensure consistency with the policies adopted by
      the Group.

      Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of                    附屬公司業績及權益內的非控股權益於綜
      subsidiaries are shown separately in the consolidated financial           合財務資料中單獨列示。
      statements.

      the companies in PRC is RMB. The consolidated financial

        improvements              useful lives and the lease terms                       期之較短者

      The assets’ residual values and useful lives are reviewed, and       資產的剩餘價值及可使用年期在各財務狀

40.8 Impairment of non-financial assets                                   40.8 非金融資產減值
      Assets that are subject to amortisation are tested for                    當發生事件或情況轉變，顯示賬面值可能無
      impairment whenever events or changes in circumstances                    法收回時，則對須攤銷的資產進行減值測試。
      indicate that the carrying amount may not be recoverable. An              減值虧損按資產賬面值超出其可收回金額
      impairment loss is recognised for the amount by which the                 的金額確認。可收回金額為資產公平值減銷
      asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable amount. The               售成本與使用價值兩者的較高者。於評估減
      recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less            值時，資產按獨立可識別現金流入的最低層
      costs to sell and value in use. For the purposes of assessing             次分組，有關現金流入大致上獨立於其他資
      impairment, assets are grouped at the lowest levels for which             產或資產組別（現金產生單位）的現金流入。
      there are separately identifiable cash inflows which are largely          在各報告期末，將審閱出現減值的非金融資
      independent of the cash inflows from other assets or groups of            產（商譽除外）是否有撥回減值的可能。
      assets (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets other than
      goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed for possible
      reversal of the impairment at the end of each reporting period.

      measurement categories:

      managing the financial assets and the contractual terms of the           式以及現金流量的合約條款。
      cash flows.

      For assets measured at fair value, gains and losses will either          對於以公平值計量的資產，其收益及虧損計
      be recorded in profit or loss or OCI. For investments in equity          入損益或其他全面收益。對於非持作買賣的
      instruments that are not held for trading, this will depend on           權益工具投資，其收益及虧損之計量將取決
      whether the Group has made an irrevocable election at the                於本集團在初始確認時是否作出不可撤銷的
      time of initial recognition to account for the equity investment         選擇，以將按公平值計入其他全面收益（「按
      at fair value through other comprehensive income (“FVOCI”).              公平值計入其他全面收益」
                                                                                          ）的權益投資入賬。

      The Group reclassifies debt investments when and only when               僅當管理該等資產之業務模式發生變化時，
      its business model for managing those assets changes.                    本集團才對債券投資進行重新分類。

      Regular way purchases and sales of financial assets are                  常規購入及出售的金融資產於交易日期（即

40.10 Offsetting financial instruments                                      40.10 抵銷金融工具
      Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount              當有法定可強制執行權利可抵銷已確認金
      reported in the consolidated statement of financial position                額，並有意按淨額基準結算或同時變現資產
      when there is a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised          及償付負債時，金融資產與負債可互相抵銷，
      amounts and there is an intention to settle on a net basis or               並於綜合財務狀況表呈報其淨額。法定可強
      realise the asset and settle the liability simultaneously. The              制執行權利不得依賴未來事件而定，而在一
      legally enforceable right must not be contingent on future                  般業務過程中以及倘本集團或對手方一旦
      events and must be enforceable in the normal course of                      出現違約、無償債能力或破產時亦必須可強
      business and in the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy              制執行。
      of the Group or the counterparty.

40.11 Inventories                                                           40.11 存貨
      Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable              存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者中的較低者
      value. Cost is determined using the weighted average method.                列賬。成本使用加權平均法釐定。採購存貨
      Costs of purchased inventory are determined after deducting                 的成本於扣除回扣及折扣後釐定。可變現淨
      rebates and discounts. Net realisable value is the estimated                值乃於日常業務過程中的估計售價減去適
      selling price in the ordinary course of business, less the                  用可變銷售開支。
      applicable variable selling expense.

40.16 Borrowings and borrowing costs                                         40.16 借款及借款成本
     Borrowings are initially recognised at fair value, net of                  借款初步按公平值確認，經扣除已產生的交
     transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently                    易成本。借款其後以攤銷成本列賬。所得款
     measured at amortised cost. Any difference between the                     項（經 扣 除 交 易 成 本）與 贖 回 金 額 之 間 的 任
     proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the redemption amount              何差額，使用實際利率法於借款期內在損益
     is recognised in profit or loss over the period of the borrowings          中確認。於建立貸款融資時支付的費用於將
     using the effective interest method. Fees paid on the                      很有可能提取部分或全部融資時確認為貸
     establishment of loan facilities are recognised as transaction             款的交易成本。於此情況下，有關費用遞延
     costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some or           至融資被提取時。倘無證據顯示將很有可能
     all of the facility will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is           提取部分或全部融資，有關費用將被資本化
     deferred until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no             為流動資金服務的預付款項並於相關融資
     evidence that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be      期間進行攤銷。
     drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a prepayment for liquidity
     services and amortised over the period of the facility to which it
     relates.

     Borrowings are removed from the balance sheet when the                     當合約中規定的責任獲解除、取消或屆滿時，
     obligation specified in the contract is discharged, cancelled              借款自資產負債表中剔除。金融負債賬面值
     or expired. The difference between the carrying amount of a               （已消除或轉撥至另一方）與已付代價（包括

     another party and the consideration paid, including any non-               間的差額於損益中確認為融資成本。
     cash assets transferred or liabilities assumed, is recognised in
     profit or loss as finance costs.

40.17 Current and deferred income tax                                       40.17 即期及遞延所得稅
      The income tax expense for the year ended 31 December                       截至 2023 年 12 月 31 日止年度的所得稅開支
      2023 comprises current and deferred tax. Tax is recognised                  包括即期稅項及遞延稅項。稅項在綜合全面
      in the consolidated statements of comprehensive income,                     收益表中確認，惟與於其他全面收益或直接
      except to the extent that it relates to items recognised in other           於權益中確認的項目相關者除外。於此情況
      comprehensive income or directly in equity. In this case,                   下，稅項亦分別於其他全面收益或直接於權
      the tax is also recognised in other comprehensive income or                 益中確認。
      directly in equity, respectively.

(a)   Current income tax                                                    (a)   即期所得稅
      The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of the             即期所得稅開支根據本集團營運所在及產

      statement of financial position date in the country where the               期已頒佈或實質已頒佈的稅法計算。管理層
      Group operates and generates taxable income. Management                     就適用稅務法例有待詮釋的情況定期評估
      periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect          報稅表的狀況，並於適用情況下根據預期須
      to situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to              向稅務機構支付的稅款設定撥備。
      interpretation. It establishes provisions where appropriate on

40.17 Current and deferred income tax (Continued)                           40.17 即期及遞延所得稅（續）
(b)   Deferred income tax                                                   (b)   遞延所得稅
      Income tax is recognised, using the liability method, on                    所得稅就資產及負債的稅基與其於綜合財
      temporary differences arising between the tax bases of assets               務報表所示賬面值之間的暫時性差額以負
      and liabilities and their carrying amounts in the consolidated              債法確認。然而，如遞延稅項負債產生自初
      financial statements. However, the deferred tax liabilities are             步確認商譽，則不會確認遞延稅項負債。倘
      not recognised if they arise from initial recognition of goodwill,          於非業務合併的交易中初步確認資產或負
      the deferred income tax is not accounted for if it is from initial          債而產生遞延所得稅，而交易當時並無影響
      recognition of an asset or liability in a transaction other than a          會計或應課稅溢利或虧損，則不會將遞延所
      business combination that at the time of the transaction affects            得稅入賬。遞延所得稅按綜合財務狀況表日
      neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred income              期前已實施或實際上已實施，並在變現有關
      tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been                 遞延所得稅資產或清償遞延所得稅負債時
      enacted or substantially enacted by the consolidated statement              預期將會適用的稅率（及法律）釐定。
      of financial position date and are expected to apply when the
      related deferred income tax asset is realised or the deferred
      income tax liability is settled.

      Deferred tax assets are recognised only if it is probable                   僅當可能有未來應課稅金額用於動用該等
      that future taxable amounts will be available to utilise those              暫時性差額及虧損時，方會確認遞延稅項資
      temporary differences and losses.                                           產。

      Deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised for                  在公司有能力控制暫時性差額的撥回時及
      temporary differences between the carrying amount and tax                   差額可能不會在可見未來撥回的情況下，不
      bases of investments in foreign operations where the company                會就外國業務投資的賬面值與稅基之間的
      is able to control the timing of the reversal of the temporary              暫時性差額確認遞延稅項資產及負債。
      differences and it is probable that the differences will not
      reverse in the foreseeable future.

      Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset where there is               倘存在可依法強制執行權利以抵銷即期稅
      a legally enforceable right to offset current tax assets and                項資產及負債，且倘遞延稅項結餘與同一稅
      liabilities and where the deferred tax balances relate to the               務機關相關，則會抵銷遞延稅項資產及負債。
      same taxation authority. Current tax assets and tax liabilities are         倘實體擁有可依法強制執行權利進行抵銷
      offset where the entity has a legally enforceable right to offset           及擬按淨額結付，或同時變現資產及結付負
      and intends either to settle on a net basis, or to realise the              債，則會抵銷即期稅項資產及稅項負債。
      asset and settle the liability simultaneously.

40.19 Provisions                                                           40.19 撥備
      Provisions are recognised when: the Group has a present                    倘本集團因過往事件而承擔現有法定或推
      legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events; it is         定責任；有可能須流出資源以履行責任；且
      probable that an outflow of resources will be required to settle           有關金額已可靠估計時，則會確認撥備。概
      the obligation; and the amount has been reliably estimated.                不會就未來經營虧損確認撥備。
      Provisions are not recognised for future operating losses.

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood            倘出現多項類似責任，履行責任時須流出資
      that an outflow will be required in settlement is determined by            源的可能性乃經考慮整體責任類別後釐定。
      considering the class of obligations as a whole. A provision is            即使就同一責任類別所包含的任何一個項
      recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to            目流出資源的可能性極低，仍須確認撥備。
      any one item included in the same class of obligations may be
      small.

      Provisions are measured at the present value of management’s               撥備按管理層對預期履行有關責任所需開
      best estimate of the expenditures expected to be required to               支的最佳估計現值採用稅前利率計量，該利
      settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate that reflects current           率反映當時市場對金錢時間值和有關責任
      market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                特定風險的評估。因時間推移而增加的撥備
      specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision due to           確認為財務成本開支。
      passage of time is recognised as finance cost expense.

            excluding any costs of servicing equity other than                        外的任何權益服務成本），除以
            ordinary shares; and

      •     by the weighted average number of ordinary shares                    •    財政年度內發行在外普通股加權平均
            outstanding during the financial year, adjusted for bonus                 數，並就年內已發行普通股（不包括庫
            elements in ordinary shares issued during the year and                    存股）的股利成分調整。
            excluding treasury shares.

Reporting Scope                                                          報告範圍
The board of directors of the Company (the “Board”) is responsible       本公司董事會（「董事會」）負責確定報告的範圍。
for determining the scope of the Report. This Report focuses on the      本報告專註於對我們的 ESG 表現有重大影響的本

substantial impact on our ESG performance, covering (1) offices          民共和國（  「中國」）(1) 昆山、北京、上海、廣州及
in Kunshan, Beijing, Shanghai, Guangzhou and Hangzhou, (2)               杭 州 的 辦 事 處，(2) 昆 山 的 倉 庫；及 (3) 昆 山 及 杭
warehouses in Kunshan, and (3) staff quarters in Kunshan and             州的員工宿舍。
Hangzhou, the People’s Republic of China (“PRC”).

The two business segments are:                                           兩個業務分部為：

(i)    New retail business segment, focusing on development and          (i)    新零售業務分部，專註於低碳水化合物健康
       sale of low-carb health management products, as well as                  管理產品以及護膚品的開發及銷售；及
       skincare products; and

(ii)   Intellectual Property (“IP”) creation and operation business      (ii)   知識產權（「IP」）創造及營運業務分部，專註
       segment, focusing on IP content creation and management                  於 IP 內容創造及管理業務，以及 IP 授權及相
       business, and IP licensing and sales of related products.                關產品銷售。

The above reporting scope of the Report was determined based on          本報告的上述報告範圍乃根據重要性原則釐定，
the principle of materiality, as the revenue from the above segments     原因為於報告期間，來自上述分部及地點的收益
and locations accounted for approximately 99.5% of the total revenue     佔本集團總收益約 99.5%，而上述分部及地點的
of the Group and the number of employees in the above segments           僱員人數佔本集團僱員總數約 90.7%。本集團將
and locations constituted approximately 90.7% of the total number        繼續檢討自身表現，並在必要時調整或擴大報告
of employees of the Group during the Reporting Period. The Group         範圍，以提高披露質素。
will continue to review its own performance and adjust or expand the
reporting scope as necessary to improve the quality of disclosures.

Governance Structure                                                    管治架構

reporting. The Board is responsible for evaluating and determining      董事會負責評估及釐定本集團的 ESG 相關風險，
the Group’s ESG-related risks and ensuring that appropriate and         並確保設有合適有效的 ESG 管理政策及內部監控
effective ESG risk management and internal control systems are          制度。本公司執行董事負責制定 ESG 管理政策、
in place. The executive directors of the Company are responsible        戰略、目標及年度報告，並推動相關實施。彼等
for formulating ESG management policies, strategies, goals, and         亦 識 別、評 估、審 查 及 管 理 重 大 ESG 議 題、風 險
annual reporting and promoting related implementation. They also        及機遇，而其他部門負責組織、推動及實施本集
identify, evaluate, review, and manage major ESG issues, risks and      團 ESG 管理政策及策略項下的多項 ESG 相關任務。
opportunities while other departments are responsible for organizing,   所有任務將定期向董事會報告，以期對照本環境、
promoting, and implementing various ESG related tasks under the         社會及管治報告報告載列環境、社會及管治相關
Group’s ESG management policies and strategies. All tasks will be       目標及指標的進展情況，並確保目標及指標與本
reported to the Board regularly in order to compare the progress        集團的戰略業務發展相輔相成，最終達到降低本

Materiality Assessment                                                       重要性評估
The Group attaches significant importance to stakeholders’ concerns          本集團重視持份者對各 ESG 議題的關注，於本年
to various ESG issues. It has commissioned an independent                    度委託獨立顧問與不同持份者進行問卷調查，數

A.   ENVIRONMENT                                                        A.   環境
     As our business does not involve manufacturing and production           由於我們的業務不涉及製造及生產活動，且
     activities, and a large portion of resources are provided by            大部分資源由我們的供應商及第三方物流
     our suppliers and third-party logistics service providers,              服務供應商提供，因此我們的營運對環境並
     therefore our operations does not have material negative                無重大負面影響。儘管如此，本集團重視在
     impacts on the environment. Nevertheless, the Group attaches            業務穩健營運的同時盡量減少其環境足跡，
     great importance to minimising its environmental footprint              推進經濟、社會及環境平衡及可持續發展。
     while maintaining stable business operations, to promote                本集團履行環境責任並嚴格遵循環境法律
     the balanced and sustainable development of economy,                    及法規，亦致力將環境保護的理念納入其營
     society and environment. The Group fulfils its environmental            運及決策過程中。
     responsibilities and strictly abides by environmental laws and
     regulations, it is also committed to incorporating the concept
     of environmental protection into its operations and decision-
     making processes.

     To effectively control its negative impact on the environment,          為有效管控本集團對環境造成的負面影響，
     the Group has continuously strengthened and improved its                本集團透過促進資源的有效使用、提高自然
     environmental management by facilitating efficient use of               資源的有效利用、減少廢棄物及污染以及應
     resources, enhancing efficient utilisation of natural resources,        對氣候變化，持續加強及完善其環境管理，
     reducing waste and pollution, and combating climate change,             以推動生態及環境保護。
     so as to promote ecological and environmental conservation.

     In order to further improve its environmental performance,              為進一步提升其環境表現，本集團將繼續以
     the Group will continue to review its own performance in a              量化方式檢討自身表現，並就制定以下環境
     quantitative manner and conduct internal discussions for                目標進行內部討論，進一步詳情將於完成內
     setting the following environmental targets, and further details        部討論後適時披露。
     will be disclosed in due course after completing internal
     discussions.

1.3   Wastes Management                                                        1.3   廢棄物管理
      Proper handling of waste is another aspect the Group has laid                  除廢氣排放控制外，妥善處理廢棄物亦為本
      emphasis on, other than air emission control. We adhere to the                 集團重視的另一方面。我們於業務營運中盡
      “3R” approach to environmental conservation, i.e. reduction                    可 能 遵 循「3R」環 保 方 針，即 減 少 廢 物、資
      of waste, reuse of resources and recycling of used materials,                  源再利用及回收廢舊材料，以示關愛環境。
      to the largest practicable extent in our business operation as a               鑑於業務性質，由於我們的營運並不涉及任
      mean of care for the environment. Due to the business nature,                  何重大生產活動，故本集團並無產生重大有
      the Group does not generate material hazardous waste because                   害廢棄物。本集團產生的無害廢棄物主要包
      our operation does not involve any significant production                      括辦公室及員工宿舍營運產生的生活垃圾
      activities. The non-hazardous waste generated by the Group                     及紙張，以及倉庫營運產生的紙箱及過期產
      consists primarily of household waste and paper from office                    品。本集團一直致力確保所有廢棄物均以合
      and staff quarter operation, and carton box and expired                        法及適當的方式處理，以避免對環境造成負
      products from warehouse operation. The Group is always                         面影響。紙張及紙箱由一般廢物收集商收集
      keen to ensure that all waste is handled and treated in a legal                及處理。儘管我們已就存貨管理建立完善的
      and appropriate way so as to avoid negative impacts on the                     內部監控，但對於偶爾出現的極少量過期產
      environment. Paper and carton box are collected and handled                    品，我們委聘合資格的廢棄物收集商按照當
      by general waste collectors. Despite having established sound                  地法規及環保標準進行處理。
      internal control for inventory management, for the occasional
      occurrence of an extremely small quantity of expired products,
      we engage qualified wastes collectors to handle in compliance
      with local regulations and environmental standard.

      To reduce non-hazardous wastes generation, we have                           為減少無害廢棄物的產生，我們已實施以下
      implemented the following measures:                                          措施：

      •        Enhance the use of online marketing and promotion                   •    更 多 地 使 用 線 上 營 銷 推 廣，降 低 線 下
               and reduce the resources consumption level of offline                    營銷活動的資源消耗水平；及
               marketing activities; and

      •        Implement the use of online system for internal                     •    推行利用線上系統處理內部行政程序
               administrative procedures to reduce the use of paper                     以 減 少 紙 質 文 件 的 使 用，同 時 促 進 雙
               documents, and avoid waste of paper by promoting                         面打印以防止紙張浪費；及
               printing on both sides; and

      •        Better co-ordinate with our suppliers and close monitor             •    更 好 地 與 供 應 商 協 調，密 切 監 控 我 們
               of our stock level to adjust the production plan and                     的 庫 存 水 平，及 時 調 整 生 產 計 劃 及 採
               purchasing plan timely thus reducing the risk of                         購 計 劃，從 而 降 低 庫 存 過 剩 的 風 險，

      •     Installation of automatic faucets; and                               •     安裝自動水龍頭；及

      risks to its business operations, including policy, reputation and         劇、海平面上升、持續熱浪以及低碳轉型所

      sea level, chronic heat waves and low-carbon transition, which             業務表現，同時增加可持續發展的不穩定性。
      may affect its business performance and add instability to its
      sustainable development.

      to further analyse the impact of relevant risks. To reduce the             響。為降低氣候變化對業務的影響，本集團
      impact of climate change on business, the Group will formulate             會於日後因應不同的風險制訂進一步應對

5.1     Employment                                                           5.1   僱傭
         Recruitment and equal opportunity                                         招聘及平等機會
         Our success depends on our ability to attract, retain and                 我們的成功取決於我們吸引、挽留及激勵合
         motivate qualified personnel. We have invested significant                資格人員的能力。我們已投入大量資源為本
         resources in the recruiting of the suitable candidates for                司招募合適候選人。我們主要透過網上渠道
         our Company. We primarily recruit our employees through                   及社交網絡平台招聘僱員，並根據《中華人
         online channels and social networking platforms and enter                 民共和國勞動合同法》與長期僱員訂立規範
         into standardized employment contracts with our permanent                 的僱傭合約。
         employees in accordance with the Labor Contract Law of the
         PRC*（《中華人民共和國勞動合同法》).

                                                                          5.4   勞工準則
5.4   Labour Standards
                                                                                本集團已建立全面的招聘程序檢查候選人
      The Group has established comprehensive recruitment
                                                                                的背景，以防止營運中出現任何童工或強制
      procedures to check the background of candidates in order to
                                                                                勞工。人力資源部在錄用求職者之前，會透
      prevent any child labour or forced labour in operation. Before
                                                                                過檢查身份證件核實求職者的年齡，確保求
      hiring any job applicant, the HR Department will verify their age
                                                                                職者的外表與身份證照片一致。為防止強制
      by inspecting their identity documents and ensure applicant’s
                                                                                勞工，我們保障僱員自由選擇就業的權利，
      appearance is consistent with the photograph on the ID card.
                                                                                並確保所有僱傭關係均屬自願。本集團及其
      To prevent forced labour, we protect the right of our employees
                                                                                僱員可因個人原因或其他原因終止僱傭合約，
      to freely choose employment and ensure that all employment
                                                                                並須給予適當通知期或支付代通知金。倘出
      relationships are voluntary. The Group and its employees may
                                                                                現不合規情況，本集團將即時採取補救行動，
      terminate the employment contract for personal reasons or
                                                                                如屬必要，將終止有關僱員的合約並向有關
      other reasons, and need to give an appropriate notice period

        Case Sharing – Being the Cooperative Unit of the National Low-Carbon Medical Technology and Industry Conference*
        ( 全國低碳醫學技術與產業大會 ) for three consecutive years
        案例分享－連續三年成為全國低碳醫學技術與產業大會合作單位


        The 5th National Low-Carbon Medical Technology and Industry Conference was successfully held on 10 December 2023.
        All along, the Group has been committed to promoting the new health concept of “MODONG New Life-style* ( 魔胴新生
        活 )”, which is a life-style of balanced nutrition, low-carbon diet, scientific body-building, staying hydrated, getting enough
        sleep and timely releasing stress.
        第五屆全國低碳醫學技術與產業大會於 2023 年 12 月 10 日成功舉辦。本集團一直以來致力於推廣「魔胴新生活」
        新健康概念。「魔胴新生活」是一種均衡營養、低碳飲食、科學健身、補充水分、充足睡眠、及時釋放壓力的生活
        方式。

        We have been serving as the cooperative unit of the conference for three consecutive years, playing an important role of
        a promoter of low-carbon diets in promoting the development of low-carbon medical technology and industry.
        我們連續三年成為大會合作單位，為推動低碳醫學技術與產業發展發揮了低碳飲食推動者的重要作用。
